title: ProForm
description: Schema-driven forms with dynamic fields, validation, and field linkage — powered by FormilyJS and shadcn/ui.
---

## Installation

<CodeTabs>

<TabsList>
  <TabsTrigger value="cli">CLI</TabsTrigger>
  <TabsTrigger value="manual">Manual</TabsTrigger>
</TabsList>

```bash
npx @draco-china/shadcn-pro@latest add pro-form
```

</TabsContent>

<TabsContent value="manual">

<Steps className="mb-0 pt-2">

<Step>Install the following dependencies:</Step>

```bash
bun add @formily/core @formily/react @formily/reactive-react date-fns lucide-react
```

<Step>Copy and paste the following code into your project.</Step>

<ComponentSource name="pro-form" title="components/pro/pro-form/index.tsx" />

<Step>Update the import paths to match your project setup.</Step>

</Steps>

</TabsContent>

</CodeTabs>

## Usage

```tsx
import { createForm, ProForm } from "@/components/pro/pro-form"

const form = createForm()
const schema = {
  type: "object",
  properties: {
    username: {
      type: "string",
      title: "Username",
      required: true,
      "x-component-props": { placeholder: "Enter username" },
    },
    email: {
      type: "string",
      title: "Email",
      required: true,
      "x-component-props": { type: "email", placeholder: "user@example.com" },
    },
  },
}

export default function BasicForm() {
  return (
    <ProForm form={form} schema={schema} onFinish={(values) => console.log(values)} />
  )
}
```

`ProForm` renders `schema` through the built-in `SchemaField`, so regular JSON schema forms do not need to import or render `<SchemaField>` manually. The schema renderer automatically fills common defaults:

- `string` → `Input`
- `number` / `integer` → `Digit`
- `boolean` → `Switch`
- `enum` → `Select`
- field schemas get `x-decorator: "FormItem"` unless explicitly configured

## Examples

### Validation

Real-time and async field validation with custom error messages.

<ComponentPreview name="pro-form-validation-demo" previewClassName="min-h-[480px]" />

### Field Linkage

Show or hide fields dynamically based on other field values.

<ComponentPreview name="pro-form-linkage-demo" previewClassName="min-h-[480px]" />

### Schema-Driven

Define your entire form with a JSON schema. You can pass the schema directly to `ProForm`:

```tsx
import { ProForm } from "@/components/pro/pro-form"

const schema = {
  type: "object",
  properties: {
    username: {
      type: "string",
      title: "Username",
      required: true,
      "x-validator": [{ required: true, message: "Username is required" }],
    },
    role: {
      type: "string",
      title: "Role",
      enum: [
        { label: "Admin", value: "admin" },
        { label: "User", value: "user" },
        { label: "Guest", value: "guest" },
      ],
    },
    notifications: {
      type: "boolean",
      title: "Enable notifications",
    },
  },
}

export default function SchemaForm() {
  return (
    <ProForm schema={schema} onFinish={(values) => console.log(values)} />
  )
}
```

Use `schemaComponents` when a schema references project-specific components:

```tsx
<ProForm
  schema={schema}
  schemaComponents={{
    UserPicker,
  }}
/>
```

### Reactions

Formily `x-reactions` are passed through unchanged, so conditional visibility and linkage can live in schema instead of React conditionals:

```tsx
const schema = {
  type: "object",
  properties: {
    type: {
      type: "string",
      title: "Type",
      enum: ["personal", "company"],
    },
    companyName: {
      type: "string",
      title: "Company Name",
      "x-reactions": {
        dependencies: ["type"],
        fulfill: {
          state: {
            visible: "{{$deps[0] === 'company'}}",
          },
        },
      },
    },
  },
}
```

### Header Submitter

```tsx
<ProForm
  submitter={{
    submit: { text: "保存", submittingText: "保存中..." },
    cancel: { text: "取消", onClick: () => router.back() },
    header: (actions) => (
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between border-b pb-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-lg font-semibold">新增套餐</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            配置套餐基础信息、价格周期和节点授权。
          </p>
        </div>
        {actions}
      </div>
    ),
  }}
  onFinish={async (values) => {
    await createPackage(values)
  }}
>
  {/* fields */}
</ProForm>
```

`submitter.header` and `submitter.footer` receive the default actions node. Use them when a page needs title, description, tabs, status, or any custom layout around the built-in submit/cancel/reset buttons.

### Array Fields

Dynamic list of items with add/remove controls:

```ts
const schema = {
  type: "object",
  properties: {
    users: {
      type: "array",
      title: "Team Members",
      "x-component": "ArrayField",
      items: {
        type: "object",
        properties: {
          name: {
            type: "string",
            title: "Name",
          },
        },
      },
    },
  },
}
```

### Object Fields

Nested object grouping with optional card wrapper:

```ts
const schema = {
  type: "object",
  properties: {
    address: {
      type: "object",
      title: "Address",
      "x-component": "ObjectField",
      properties: {
        city: { type: "string", title: "City" },
      },
    },
  },
}
```

### Modal Form

Open a form inside a Dialog. Pass a `trigger` element for uncontrolled usage, or omit `trigger` and control it with `open` / `onOpenChange`. `schema` and `schemaComponents` are supported the same way as `ProForm`.

<ComponentPreview name="pro-form-modal-demo" previewClassName="min-h-[280px]" />

### Drawer Form

Same as Modal Form but rendered inside a side Drawer. Use the `side` prop to control which edge it slides in from.

<ComponentPreview name="pro-form-drawer-demo" previewClassName="min-h-[280px]" />

### Field Tooltip and Extra

`FormItem` follows Formily's decorator semantics: `tooltip` renders as the info icon beside the label, while `extra` renders helper text below the field. Schema `description` also renders as the label tooltip, and an explicit `tooltip` takes priority.

```tsx
<ProForm
  schema={{
    type: "object",
    properties: {
      email: {
        type: "string",
        title: "Email",
        required: true,
        description: "We will never share your email with anyone.",
        "x-component-props": { placeholder: "user@example.com" },
      },
      bio: {
        type: "string",
        title: "Bio",
        "x-component": "Textarea",
        "x-decorator-props": {
          tooltip: "Shown on your public profile.",
          extra: "Max 200 characters.",
        },
      },
    },
  }}
/>
```

### Manual SchemaField

`SchemaField` is still exported for advanced JSX composition or local recursive rendering. Prefer `ProForm schema={schema}` for normal JSON schema forms.

## API Reference

### ProForm

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `form` | `Form` | internal form | Formily form instance created with `createForm()`. Omit to let `ProForm` create one. |
| `formProps` | `IFormProps` | — | Options used when `ProForm` creates the internal form. |
| `schema` | `ISchema` | — | JSON schema rendered by the built-in schema field. Defaults are inferred for common field types. |
| `schemaComponents` | `SchemaReactComponents` | — | Extra components available to JSON schema. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Form fields and layout components. |
| `onFinish` | `(values) => void \| Promise<void>` | — | Called after successful validation. |
| `onFinishFailed` | `(errors) => void` | — | Called when validation fails on submit. |
| `onReset` | `() => void \| Promise<void>` | — | Called after reset completes. |
| `submitter` | `false \| ProFormSubmitterProps` | `{}` | Built-in submitter config. Set to `false` to hide actions. |
| `columns` | `1 \| 2 \| 3 \| 4` | — | Multi-column layout. Wraps fields in `ProFormGrid` automatically. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Additional className for the `<form>` element. |

### ProFormSubmitter

Object config for the built-in submit/cancel/reset area. `header` renders above the form and `footer` renders below it; both receive the default actions node.

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `position` | `'header' \| 'footer'` | `'footer'` | Where default actions render when no `header` slot is provided. |
| `submit` | `false \| ProFormSubmitActionProps` | `{}` | Submit button config. Set `false` or `hidden: true` to hide it. |
| `cancel` | `false \| ProFormCancelActionProps` | — | Cancel button config. Omit to hide it. |
| `reset` | `false \| ProFormResetActionProps` | — | Reset button config. Omit to hide it. |
| `submitting` | `boolean` | — | Controlled submitting state for default actions. |
| `loading` | `boolean` | `false` | Fallback loading state for default actions. |
| `actionsVariant` | `'page' \| 'inline' \| 'overlay'` | header: `'page'`, footer: `'inline'` | Visual variant for actions. |
| `align` | `'left' \| 'center' \| 'right'` | header: `'right'`, footer: `'left'` | Alignment of default actions. |
| `header` | `ReactNode \| (actions, context) => ReactNode` | — | Custom header submitter slot. |
| `footer` | `ReactNode \| (actions, context) => ReactNode` | default footer renderer | Custom footer submitter slot. |

### ProFormGrid

Multi-column grid container — fields inside are evenly distributed.

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `columns` | `1 \| 2 \| 3 \| 4` | `1` | Number of columns. |
| `gap` | `string` | `'gap-4'` | Tailwind gap class. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Additional className. |

### ProFormActions

Standalone action bar — use directly when a page needs to compose actions outside `ProForm`.

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `submit` | `false \| ProFormSubmitActionProps` | `{}` | Submit button config. |
| `cancel` | `false \| ProFormCancelActionProps` | — | Cancel button config. |
| `reset` | `false \| ProFormResetActionProps` | — | Reset button config. |
| `submitting` | `boolean` | — | Controlled submitting state. |
| `loading` | `boolean` | `false` | Fallback loading state. |
| `actionsVariant` | `'page' \| 'inline' \| 'overlay'` | `'inline'` | Visual variant for page header, inline, or overlay footer actions. |
| `align` | `'left' \| 'center' \| 'right'` | `'left'` | Alignment of buttons. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Extra buttons or content to render alongside actions. |

### ModalForm

A `ProForm` embedded inside a shadcn `Dialog`. The dialog opens when the `trigger` is clicked and closes automatically on successful submit.

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `trigger` | `ReactNode` | — | Optional element that opens the dialog on click. Omit for controlled open mode. |
| `title` | `string` | — | Dialog title. |
| `description` | `string` | — | Optional subtitle shown below the title. |
| `schema` | `ISchema` | — | JSON schema rendered inside the form. |
| `schemaComponents` | `SchemaReactComponents` | — | Extra schema components. |
| `open` | `boolean` | — | Controlled open state. |
| `onOpenChange` | `(open: boolean) => void` | — | Controlled open state handler. |
| `onFinish` | `(values) => void \| Promise<void>` | — | Called after successful validation. Dialog closes on resolve. |
| `onFinishFailed` | `(errors) => void` | — | Called when validation fails. Dialog stays open. |
| `submitter` | `false \| OverlayFormSubmitterProps` | `{}` | Submitter config. Set `false` to hide footer actions. |
| `form` | `Form` | internal | Formily form instance. |
| `formProps` | `IFormProps` | — | Options when creating the internal form. |
| `columns` | `1 \| 2 \| 3 \| 4` | — | Multi-column layout. |
| `widthClass` | `string` | `'sm:max-w-lg'` | Tailwind class controlling dialog width. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Additional className for the form element. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Form fields (`SchemaField` nodes or layout components). |

### DrawerForm

Identical to `ModalForm` but renders inside a shadcn `Drawer` that slides in from a screen edge.

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `trigger` | `ReactNode` | — | Optional element that opens the drawer on click. Omit for controlled open mode. |
| `title` | `string` | — | Drawer title. |
| `description` | `string` | — | Optional subtitle shown below the title. |
| `schema` | `ISchema` | — | JSON schema rendered inside the form. |
| `schemaComponents` | `SchemaReactComponents` | — | Extra schema components. |
| `side` | `'right' \| 'left' \| 'top' \| 'bottom'` | `'right'` | Which edge the drawer slides in from. |
| `open` | `boolean` | — | Controlled open state. |
| `onOpenChange` | `(open: boolean) => void` | — | Controlled open state handler. |
| `onFinish` | `(values) => void \| Promise<void>` | — | Called after successful validation. Drawer closes on resolve. |
| `onFinishFailed` | `(errors) => void` | — | Called when validation fails. Drawer stays open. |
| `submitter` | `false \| OverlayFormSubmitterProps` | `{}` | Submitter config. Set `false` to hide footer actions. |
| `form` | `Form` | internal | Formily form instance. |
| `formProps` | `IFormProps` | — | Options when creating the internal form. |
| `columns` | `1 \| 2 \| 3 \| 4` | — | Multi-column layout. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Additional className for the form element. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Form fields (`SchemaField` nodes or layout components). |

import { createForm } from '@formily/core'
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'

import { ProForm, ProFormGrid, SchemaField } from '@/components/pro/pro-form/index'

export function ProFormDemo() {
  const form = useMemo(() => createForm(), [])
  const [result, setResult] = useState<Record<string, unknown> | null>(null)

  async function handleFinish(values: Record<string, unknown>) {
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 800))
    setResult(values)
  }

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-2xl space-y-6 p-6">
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Team Member Registration</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          A comprehensive form demonstrating all ProForm field types.
        </p>
      </div>

      <ProForm
        form={form}
        onFinish={handleFinish}
        submitter={{ submit: { text: "Submit" }, reset: { text: "Reset" } }}
      >
        <SchemaField>
        {/* Basic info — ObjectField */}
        <SchemaField.Object
          name="basic"
          title="Basic Information"
          x-component="ObjectField"
          x-component-props={{ variant: 'separated', collapsible: true }}
        >
          <ProFormGrid columns={2}>
            <SchemaField.String
              name="firstName"
              title="First Name"
              required
              x-decorator="FormItem"
              x-component="Input"
              x-component-props={{ placeholder: 'First name' }}
            />
            <SchemaField.String
              name="lastName"
              title="Last Name"
              required
              x-decorator="FormItem"
              x-component="Input"
              x-component-props={{ placeholder: 'Last name' }}
            />
            <SchemaField.String
              name="email"
              title="Email"
              required
              x-validator="email"
              x-decorator="FormItem"
              x-component="Input"
              x-component-props={{ placeholder: 'user@example.com' }}
            />
            <SchemaField.String
              name="password"
              title="Password"
              required
              x-decorator="FormItem"
              x-component="Password"
              x-component-props={{ placeholder: 'Enter password' }}
            />
          </ProFormGrid>
          <SchemaField.String
            name="bio"
            title="Bio"
            description="Up to 200 characters."
            x-decorator="FormItem"
            x-component="Textarea"
            x-component-props={{ placeholder: 'Tell us about yourself', rows: 3 }}
          />

        {/* Role & Settings */}

          x-decorator="FormItem"

        >
          <SchemaField.Object>
            <ProFormGrid columns={2}>
              <SchemaField.String
                name="name"
                title="Name"
                required
                x-decorator="FormItem"

              />
              <SchemaField.String
                name="phone"
                title="Phone"
                x-decorator="FormItem"
                x-component="Input"
                x-component-props={{ placeholder: '+1 (555) 000-0000' }}
              />
            </ProFormGrid>
          </SchemaField.Object>
        </SchemaField.Array>

        {/* Avatar upload */}
        <SchemaField.String
          name="avatar"
          title="Avatar"
          description="Upload a profile picture (PNG, JPG, max 2MB)."
          x-decorator="FormItem"
          x-component="Upload"
          x-component-props={{ accept: 'image/*', maxFiles: 1 }}
        />

        <SchemaField.Boolean
          name="agree"
          title=" "
          required
          x-decorator="FormItem"
          x-component="Checkbox"
          x-content="I agree to the Terms of Service and Privacy Policy"
        />
        </SchemaField>
      </ProForm>

      {result && (
        <div className="rounded-md border bg-muted/40 p-4">
          <p className="mb-2 text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase">Submitted values</p>
          <pre className="text-xs text-foreground overflow-auto max-h-48">
            {JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)}
          </pre>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

82:Tb73,'use client'

import { createForm } from '@formily/core'
import { useMemo } from 'react'
import { ProForm } from '@/components/pro/pro-form/index'

const schema = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    email: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Email',
      required: true,
      'x-component-props': {
        placeholder: 'your@email.com',
      },
      'x-validator': [
        { required: true, message: 'Email is required' },
        { format: 'email', message: 'Please enter a valid email address' },
      ],
    },
    password: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Password',
      required: true,
      'x-component': 'Password',
      'x-component-props': {
        placeholder: 'At least 8 characters',
      },
      'x-validator': [
        { required: true, message: 'Password is required' },
        { min: 8, message: 'Password must be at least 8 characters' },
      ],
    },
    confirmPassword: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Confirm Password',
      required: true,
      'x-component': 'Password',
      'x-component-props': {
        placeholder: 'Re-enter password',
      },
      'x-validator': [
        { required: true, message: 'Please confirm your password' },
        {
          validator: (
            value: string,
            rule: unknown,
            ctx: { field: { query: (path: string) => { value: () => string } } },
          ) => {
            void rule
            const pwd = ctx.field.query('password').value()
            if (value !== pwd) return 'Passwords do not match'
            return ''
          },
        },
      ],
    },
    role: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Role',
      required: true,
      enum: [
        { label: 'Admin', value: 'admin' },
        { label: 'Editor', value: 'editor' },
        { label: 'User', value: 'user' },
      ],
      'x-component-props': {
        placeholder: 'Select a role',
      },
      'x-validator': [{ required: true, message: 'Please select a role' }],
    },
    agree: {
      type: 'boolean',
      title: 'Agree to Terms of Service',
      required: true,
      'x-component': 'Checkbox',
      'x-validator': [
        {
          validator: (value: boolean) => (value ? '' : 'You must agree to the terms of service'),
        },
      ],
    },
  },
}

export function ProFormValidationDemo() {
  const form = useMemo(
    () =>
      createForm({
        validateFirst: true,
      }),
    [],
  )

  async function handleFinish(values: Record<string, unknown>) {
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 800))
    alert(`Submitted successfully:\n${JSON.stringify(values, null, 2)}`)
  }

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md p-6">
      <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-semibold">Create Account</h2>
      <ProForm
        form={form}
        schema={schema}
        onFinish={handleFinish}
        submitter={{ submit: { text: "Register" }, reset: {} }}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

import { createForm } from '@formily/core'
import { useField } from '@formily/react'
import { observer } from '@formily/reactive-react'
import { useMemo } from 'react'
import { ProForm } from '@/components/pro/pro-form/index'

// Enterprise-specific field: listens to the `type` field to conditionally show/hide
const CompanyField = observer(() => {
  const field = useField()
  if (field.display === 'none' || field.display === 'hidden') return null
  return null
})
CompanyField.displayName = 'CompanyField'

const schema = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    type: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Account Type',
      required: true,
      enum: [
        { label: 'Personal', value: 'personal' },
        { label: 'Enterprise', value: 'enterprise' },
      ],
      'x-component-props': { placeholder: 'Select account type' },
      'x-validator': [{ required: true, message: 'Please select an account type' }],
    },
    company: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Company Name',
      'x-component-props': { placeholder: 'Enter full company name' },
      'x-validator': [{ required: true, message: 'Company name is required' }],
      'x-reactions': {
        dependencies: ['type'],
        fulfill: {
          state: {
            visible: '{{$deps[0] === "enterprise"}}',
            required: '{{$deps[0] === "enterprise"}}',
          },
        },
      },
    },
    name: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Name',
      required: true,
      'x-component-props': { placeholder: 'Enter your name' },
      'x-validator': [{ required: true, message: 'Name is required' }],
    },
    email: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Email',
      required: true,
      'x-component-props': { placeholder: 'your@email.com' },
      'x-validator': [
        { required: true, message: 'Email is required' },
        { format: 'email', message: 'Please enter a valid email' },
      ],
    },
    notifications: {
      type: 'boolean',
      title: 'Subscribe to Email Notifications',
    },
  },
}

export function ProFormLinkageDemo() {
  const form = useMemo(
    () =>
      createForm({
        effects() {
          // Formily reactions: use x-reactions declaratively instead of manual effects
        },
      }),
    [],
  )

  async function handleFinish(values: Record<string, unknown>) {
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600))
    alert(`Submitted successfully:\n${JSON.stringify(values, null, 2)}`)
  }

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md p-6">
      <h2 className="mb-1 text-lg font-semibold">Registration Type Linkage</h2>
      <p className="mb-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        After selecting an "Enterprise" account, the Company Name field appears automatically (driven by Formily x-reactions)
      </p>
      <ProForm
        form={form}
        schema={schema}
        onFinish={handleFinish}
        submitter={{ submit: { text: "Submit" }, reset: {} }}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

dd:T729,'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { ModalForm } from '@/components/pro/pro-form/index'

const schema = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    name: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Name',
      required: true,
      'x-component-props': { placeholder: 'Full name' },
    },
    email: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Email',
      required: true,
      'x-validator': 'email',
      'x-component-props': { placeholder: 'user@example.com' },
    },
    role: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Role',
      required: true,
      enum: [
        { label: 'Admin', value: 'admin' },
        { label: 'Developer', value: 'developer' },
        { label: 'Viewer', value: 'viewer' },
      ],
      'x-component-props': {
        placeholder: 'Select a role',
      },
    },
    active: {
      type: 'boolean',
      title: 'Active',
      default: true,
    },
  },
}

export function ProFormModalDemo() {
  const [result, setResult] = useState<Record<string, unknown> | null>(null)

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-6 p-6">
      <ModalForm
        trigger={<Button>New Member</Button>}
        title="Add Team Member"
        description="Fill in the details below to add a new team member."
        submitter={{ submit: { text: "Add Member" } }}
        schema={schema}
        onFinish={async (values) => {
          await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 800))
          setResult(values)
        }}
      />

      {result && (
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-md border bg-muted/40 p-4">
          <p className="mb-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase text-muted-foreground">Submitted</p>
          <pre className="text-xs">{JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)}</pre>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

e1:T8ca,'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { DrawerForm } from '@/components/pro/pro-form/index'

const schema = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    name: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Full Name',
      required: true,
      'x-component-props': { placeholder: 'Your name' },
    },
    bio: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Bio',
      'x-component': 'Textarea',
      'x-component-props': { placeholder: 'Tell us about yourself', rows: 4 },
    },
    role: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Role',
      enum: [
        { label: 'Admin', value: 'admin' },
        { label: 'Developer', value: 'developer' },
        { label: 'Designer', value: 'designer' },
        { label: 'Viewer', value: 'viewer' },
      ],
      'x-component-props': {
        placeholder: 'Select role',
      },
    },
    start_date: {
      type: 'string',
      title: 'Start Date',
      'x-component': 'DatePicker',
      'x-component-props': { placeholder: 'Pick a date' },
    },
    rating: {
      type: 'number',
      title: 'Self Rating',
      'x-component': 'Rate',
      'x-component-props': { count: 5 },
    },
    notifications: {
      type: 'boolean',
      title: 'Notifications',
      default: true,
      description: 'Receive email notifications.',
    },
  },
}

export function ProFormDrawerDemo() {
  const [result, setResult] = useState<Record<string, unknown> | null>(null)

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-6 p-6">
      <DrawerForm
        trigger={<Button variant="outline">Edit Profile</Button>}
        title="Edit Profile"
        description="Update your profile information."
        submitter={{ submit: { text: "Save Changes" } }}
        side="right"
        schema={schema}
        onFinish={async (values) => {
          await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 800))
          setResult(values)
        }}
      />

      {result && (
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-md border bg-muted/40 p-4">
          <p className="mb-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase text-muted-foreground">Saved</p>
          <pre className="text-xs">{JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)}</pre>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

import { createForm, type Form, type IFormProps } from '@formily/core'
import { FormProvider, type SchemaReactComponents } from '@formily/react'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import * as React from 'react'
import { ProFormActions, type ProFormActionsProps, ProFormGrid } from './layout'
import { createSchemaFieldWithComponents, type ProFormSchema, SchemaField } from './schema'

export type { ProFormActionsProps, ProFormLayoutProps } from './layout'
export { DrawerForm, ModalForm } from './overlay-form'
export type { Form, IFormProps }
export { createForm, createSchemaFieldWithComponents, ProFormActions, ProFormGrid, SchemaField }

export interface ProFormRenderContext {
  form: Form
  submitting: boolean
  submit: () => void
  reset: () => void | Promise<void>
}

export type ProFormSubmitterSlot =
  | ReactNode
  | ((actions: ReactNode, context: ProFormRenderContext) => ReactNode)

export interface ProFormSubmitterProps extends ProFormActionsProps {
  position?: 'header' | 'footer'
  header?: ProFormSubmitterSlot
  footer?: ProFormSubmitterSlot
}

export interface ProFormProps {
  form?: Form
  formProps?: IFormProps
  schema?: ProFormSchema
  schemaComponents?: SchemaReactComponents
  children?: ReactNode
  onFinish?: (values: Record<string, unknown>) => void | Promise<void>
  onFinishFailed?: (errors: unknown) => void
  onReset?: () => void | Promise<void>
  /** Submitter config. Set to false to hide built-in actions. */
  submitter?: false | ProFormSubmitterProps
  /** Number of form columns (passed through to ProFormGrid) */
  columns?: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4
  className?: string
}

export function ProForm({
  form,
  formProps,
  schema,
  schemaComponents,
  children,
  onFinish,
  onFinishFailed,
  onReset,
  submitter,
  columns,
  className,
}: ProFormProps) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(false)
  const internalFormRef = React.useRef<Form | null>(null)

  if (!internalFormRef.current) {
    internalFormRef.current = createForm(formProps)
  }

  const activeForm = form ?? internalFormRef.current
  const ActiveSchemaField = React.useMemo(
    () => createSchemaFieldWithComponents(schemaComponents),
    [schemaComponents],
  )
  const activeSubmitter = submitter === false ? undefined : (submitter ?? {})
  const isSubmitting = activeSubmitter?.submitting ?? loading

  const handleReset = React.useCallback(async () => {
    await activeForm.reset()
    await onReset?.()
  }, [activeForm, onReset])

  const submit = React.useCallback(async () => {
    if (isSubmitting) return

    setLoading(true)
    try {
      await activeForm.validate()
      await onFinish?.(activeForm.values)
    } catch (err) {
      onFinishFailed?.(err)
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [activeForm, isSubmitting, onFinish, onFinishFailed])

  function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    void submit()
  }

  const activeActionsPlacement = activeSubmitter?.header
    ? 'header'
    : (activeSubmitter?.position ?? 'footer')
  const {
    position: _position,
    header: submitterHeader,
    footer: submitterFooter,
    ...submitterActions
  } = activeSubmitter ?? {}
  const renderContext = React.useMemo<ProFormRenderContext>(
    () => ({
      form: activeForm,
      submitting: isSubmitting,
      submit,
      reset: handleReset,
    }),
    [activeForm, handleReset, isSubmitting, submit],
  )
  const baseActionsProps: ProFormActionsProps = {
    ...submitterActions,
    submitting: submitterActions.submitting ?? isSubmitting,
    reset:
      submitterActions.reset === false
        ? false
        : submitterActions.reset
          ? {
              ...submitterActions.reset,
              onClick: submitterActions.reset.onClick ?? handleReset,
            }
          : undefined,
    actionsVariant:
      submitterActions.actionsVariant ?? (activeActionsPlacement === 'header' ? 'page' : 'inline'),
    align: submitterActions.align ?? (activeActionsPlacement === 'header' ? 'right' : 'left'),
  }

  const actionsNode = activeSubmitter ? <ProFormActions {...baseActionsProps} /> : null
  const headerSubmitterNode = renderSubmitterSlot(
    submitterHeader,
    actionsNode,
    renderContext,
    defaultHeaderSubmitter,
  )
  const footerSubmitterNode = renderSubmitterSlot(
    submitterFooter,
    actionsNode,
    renderContext,
    defaultFooterSubmitter,
  )

  const formContent = (
    <>
      {schema && <ActiveSchemaField schema={schema} />}
      {children}
    </>
  )

  const body = columns ? (
    <ProFormGrid columns={columns} className="mb-4">
      {formContent}
    </ProFormGrid>
  ) : (
    <div className="space-y-4">{formContent}</div>
  )

  return (
    <FormProvider form={activeForm}>
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className={className}>
        {activeSubmitter && activeActionsPlacement === 'header' && headerSubmitterNode}
        {body}
        {activeSubmitter && activeActionsPlacement === 'footer' && footerSubmitterNode}
      </form>
    </FormProvider>
  )
}

function renderSubmitterSlot(
  slot: ProFormSubmitterSlot | undefined,
  actions: ReactNode,
  context: ProFormRenderContext,
  defaultRender: (actions: ReactNode, context: ProFormRenderContext) => ReactNode,
) {
  if (!slot) return defaultRender(actions, context)
  return typeof slot === 'function' ? slot(actions, context) : slot
}

function defaultHeaderSubmitter(actions: ReactNode) {
  return <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-end gap-3 border-b pb-2">{actions}</div>
}

function defaultFooterSubmitter(actions: ReactNode) {
  return actions
}
